from re_arc.dsl import *
def generate_22233c11() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}, diff_ub: {diff_ub}")
    
    cols = remove(8, interval(0, 10, 1))
    print(f"cols: {cols}")
    
    h = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (5, 30))
    print(f"h: {h}")
    
    w = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (5, 30))
    print(f"w: {w}")
    
    nobjs = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, (h * w) // 10))
    print(f"nobjs: {nobjs}")
    
    succ = 0
    tr = 0
    maxtr = 10 * nobjs
    print(f"maxtr: {maxtr}")
    
    bgc = choice(cols)
    print(f"bgc: {bgc}")
    
    remcols = remove(bgc, cols)
    print(f"remcols: {remcols}")
    
    gi = canvas(bgc, (h, w))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")
    
    inds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"inds: {inds}")
    
    fullinds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"fullinds: {fullinds}")
    
    ncols = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, 8))
    print(f"ncols: {ncols}")
    
    ccols = sample(remcols, ncols)
    print(f"ccols: {ccols}")
    
    while succ < nobjs and tr < maxtr:
        if len(inds) == 0:
            break
        tr += 1
        print(f"tr: {tr}")
        
        od = randint(1, 3)
        print(f"od: {od}")
        
        fulld = 4 * od
        print(f"fulld: {fulld}")
        
        g = canvas(bgc, (4, 4))
        print(f"g: {g}")
        
        g = fill(g, 8, {(0, 3), (3, 0)})
        print(f"g after fill: {g}")
        
        col = choice(ccols)
        print(f"col: {col}")
        
        g = fill(g, col, {(1, 1), (2, 2)})
        print(f"g after fill with col: {g}")
        
        if choice((True, False)):
            g = hmirror(g)
            print(f"g after hmirror: {g}")
        
        g = upscale(g, od)
        print(f"g after upscale: {g}")
        
        inobj = recolor(col, ofcolor(g, col))
        print(f"inobj: {inobj}")
        
        outobj = inobj | recolor(8, ofcolor(g, 8))
        print(f"outobj: {outobj}")
        
        loc = choice(totuple(inds))
        print(f"loc: {loc}")
        
        outobj = shift(outobj, loc)
        print(f"outobj after shift: {outobj}")
        
        inobj = shift(inobj, loc)
        print(f"inobj after shift: {inobj}")
        
        outobji = toindices(outobj)
        print(f"outobji: {outobji}")
        
        if toindices(inobj).issubset(inds) and (outobji & fullinds).issubset(inds):
            succ += 1
            print(f"succ: {succ}")
            inds = (inds - outobji) - mapply(neighbors, outobji)
            print(f"inds after update: {inds}")
            gi = paint(gi, inobj)
            print(f"gi after paint: {gi}")
    
    return {'input': gi}
